  uppercase: boolean;\n    lowercase: boolean;\n    number: boolean;\n    specialChar: boolean;\n  };\n} {\n  const requirements = {\n    minLength: password.length >= 8,\n    uppercase: Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));\n  return hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');\n}\n\nexport async function verifyPassword(password: string, hash: string): {"version":3,"sources":["file:///home/z/my-project/src/lib/subscription/service.ts"],"sourcesContent":["import { db } from '@/lib/db';\nimport { FEATURE_KEYS, FEATURE_USAGE_MAP, type FeatureKey } from './features';\n\n/**\n * Get a user's effective plan (defaults to Free if no subscription exists).\n * Creates a default subscription + usage record on first access.\n */\nexport async function getUserPlan(userId: string) {\n  // Ensure user has a subscription (default to Free)\n  let subscription = await db.subscription.findUnique({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n\n  if (!subscription) {\n    const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n    if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found in database');\n\n    subscription = await db.subscription.create({\n      data: {\n        user_id: userId,\n        plan_id: freePlan.id,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      },\n      include: { plan: true },\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Ensure user has a usage record\n  const existingUsage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n  if (!existingUsage) {\n    await db.userUsage.create({ data: { user_id: userId } });\n  }\n\n  return subscription;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get plan features for a specific plan ID.\n */\nexport async function getPlanFeatures(planId: string) {\n  return db.planFeature.findMany({\n    where: { plan_id: planId },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a user can access a specific feature.\n * Returns { allowed: boolean, reason?: string, limit?: number, used?: number }\n */\nexport async function canAccess(\n  userId: string,\n  featureKey: FeatureKey\n): Promise<{\n  allowed: boolean;\n  reason?: string;\n  limit: number;\n  used: number;\n  planName: string;\n}> {\n  const subscription = await getUserPlan(userId);\n  const features = await getPlanFeatures(subscription.plan_id);\n\n  const feature = features.find(f => f.feature_key === featureKey);\n\n  // Feature not configured for this plan = not allowed\n  if (!feature || !feature.enabled) {\n    return {\n      allowed: false,\n      reason: subscription.plan.name === 'free' ? 'upgrade_required' : 'feature_not_available',\n      limit: 0,\n      used: 0,\n      planName: subscription.plan.name,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Unlimited access\n  if (feature.limit_value === -1) {\n    return {\n      allowed: true,\n      limit: -1,\n      used: 0,\n      planName: subscription.plan.name,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Check usage limit\n  const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[featureKey];\n  let used = 0;\n\n  if (usageField) {\n    const usage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n    if (usage) {\n      used = usage[usageField] ?? 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const allowed = used < feature.limit_value;\n\n  return {\n    allowed,\n    reason: allowed ? undefined : 'limit_reached',\n    limit: feature.limit_value,\n    used,\n    planName: subscription.plan.name,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a user can use a feature and optionally increment usage.\n * Returns the access result, and optionally increments the usage counter.\n */\nexport async function checkAndIncrementUsage(\n  userId: string,\n  featureKey: FeatureKey,\n  increment = true\n) {\n  const access = await canAccess(userId, featureKey);\n\n  if (!access.allowed) return access;\n\n  if (increment) {\n    const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[featureKey];\n    if (usageField) {\n      await db.userUsage.update({\n        where: { user_id: userId },\n        data: { [usageField]: { increment: 1 } },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return access;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get current usage for a user.\n */\nexport async function getUserUsage(userId: string) {\n  const subscription = await getUserPlan(userId);\n  const features = await getPlanFeatures(subscription.plan_id);\n  const usage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n\n  const featureUsage: Record<string, { limit: number; used: number; enabled: boolean }> = {};\n\n  for (const feature of features) {\n    const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[feature.feature_key];\n    const used = usage && usageField ? (usage[usageField] ?? 0) : 0;\n\n    featureUsage[feature.feature_key] = {\n      limit: feature.limit_value,\n      used,\n      enabled: feature.enabled,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    plan: {\n      id: subscription.plan.id,\n      name: subscription.plan.name,\n      display_name: subscription.plan.display_name,\n    },\n    subscription: {\n      status: subscription.status,\n      started_at: subscription.started_at,\n      expires_at: subscription.expires_at,\n      billing_cycle: subscription.billing_cycle,\n    },\n    usage: featureUsage,\n    raw: usage ?? {\n      resume_count: 0,\n      cover_letter_count: 0,\n      ai_requests: 0,\n      resume_reviews: 0,\n      exports_count: 0,\n      ats_checks: 0,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Get all active plans with their features.\n */\nexport async function getPlans() {\n  return db.plan.findMany({\n    where: { is_active: true },\n    include: { plan_features: true },\n    orderBy: { sort_order: 'asc' },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Upgrade a user's subscription to a new plan.\n */\nexport async function upgradeSubscription(\n  userId: string,\n  newPlanId: string,\n  billingCycle: 'monthly' | 'yearly' = 'monthly'\n) {\n  const newPlan = await db.plan.findUnique({ where: { id: newPlanId } });\n  if (!newPlan) throw new Error('Plan not found');\n\n  const price = billingCycle === 'yearly' ? newPlan.price_yearly : newPlan.price_monthly;\n  const now = new Date();\n  const periodEnd = new Date(now);\n  if (billingCycle === 'yearly') {\n    periodEnd.setFullYear(periodEnd.getFullYear() + 1);\n  } else {\n    periodEnd.setMonth(periodEnd.getMonth() + 1);\n  }\n\n  const existing = await db.subscription.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n\n  if (existing) {\n    return db.subscription.update({\n      where: { user_id: userId },\n      data: {\n        plan_id: newPlanId,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: billingCycle,\n        current_period_start: now,\n        current_period_end: periodEnd,\n        expires_at: periodEnd,\n        cancelled_at: null,\n        updated_at: now,\n      },\n      include: { plan: true },\n    });\n  }\n\n  return db.subscription.create({\n    data: {\n      user_id: userId,\n      plan_id: newPlanId,\n      status: 'active',\n      billing_cycle: billingCycle,\n      current_period_start: now,\n      current_period_end: periodEnd,\n      expires_at: periodEnd,\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Cancel a user's subscription (keep active until end of billing period).\n */\nexport async function cancelSubscription(userId: string) {\n  const subscription = await db.subscription.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n  if (!subscription) throw new Error('No subscription found');\n\n  if (subscription.plan.name === 'free') {\n    throw new Error('Cannot cancel free plan');\n  }\n\n  return db.subscription.update({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    data: {\n      status: 'cancelled',\n      cancelled_at: new Date(),\n      // Keep access until end of current period\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Downgrade a user to the free plan.\n */\nexport async function downgradeToFree(userId: string) {\n  const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n  if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found');\n\n  return db.subscription.update({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    data: {\n      plan_id: freePlan.id,\n      status: 'active',\n      billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      current_period_start: new Date(),\n      current_period_end: null,\n      expires_at: null,\n      cancelled_at: null,\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Initialize usage for a new user (called after signup).\n */\nexport async function initializeUsage(userId: string) {\n  const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n  if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found');\n\n  await db.$transaction([\n    db.subscription.create({\n      data: {\n        user_id: userId,\n        plan_id: freePlan.id,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      },\n    }),\n    db.userUsage.create({\n      data: { user_id: userId },\n    }),\n  ]);\n}\n\n// Quick helper: is user on free plan?\nexport async function Remove all filler words.',\n};\n\nconst BASE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are an expert resume writer and career coach with deep knowledge of ATS (Applicant Tracking System) optimization. Your task is to help users write stronger, more professional, ATS-friendly resumes. Always:\n- Use strong action verbs (Led, Developed, Implemented, Achieved, Optimized, etc.)\n- Include measurable achievements where possible (numbers, percentages, dollar amounts)\n- Keep content concise and impactful\n- Optimize for ATS keywords and formatting\n- Avoid first-person pronouns\n- Use consistent tense (past tense for past roles, present for current)\n- Return ONLY the improved text, no explanations or markdown formatting unless specifically asked`;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ntype Section = (typeof VALID_SECTIONS)[number];\ntype Action = (typeof VALID_ACTIONS)[number];\n\ninterface AiAssistBody {\n  section: string;\n  action: string;\n  content?: string;\n  itemId?: string;\n  customPrompt?: string;\n  tone?: string;\n  resumeContext?: ResumeContext;\n  field?: string;\n}\n\ninterface ResumeContext {\n  personalInfo?: {\n    fullName?: string;\n    professionalTitle?: string;\n    email?: string;\n    phone?: string;\n    location?: string;\n    website?: string;\n    linkedin?: string;\n    github?: string;\n    portfolio?: string;\n  };\n  professionalSummary?: string;\n  workExperience?: Array<{\n    id?: string;\n    position?: string;\n    company?: string;\n    location?: string;\n    startDate?: string;\n    endDate?: string;\n    current?: boolean;\n    description?: string;\n  }>;\n  education?: Array<{\n    id?: string;\n    degree?: string;\n    field?: string;\n    institution?: string;\n    location?: string;\n    startDate?: string;\n    endDate?: string;\n    gpa?: string;\n    description?: string;\n  }>;\n  skills?: Array<{ id?: string; name?: string }>;\n  projects?: Array<{\n    id?: string;\n    name?: string;\n    description?: string;\n    technologies?: string[];\n    startDate?: string;\n    endDate?: string;\n    url?: string;\n  }>;\n  certifications?: Array<{\n    name?: string;\n    issuer?: string;\n    date?: string;\n  }>;\n  languages?: Array<{\n    name?: string;\n    proficiency?: string;\n  }>;\n}\n\ninterface PromptResult {\n  systemPrompt: string;\n  userMessage: string;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Prompt Builder\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction buildPrompt(\n  section: string,\n  action: string,\n  body: AiAssistBody\n): PromptResult {\n  const { content, customPrompt, resumeContext, itemId, field } = body;\n  const ctx = resumeContext;\n\n  // Extract commonly needed context values\n  const jobTitle = ctx?.personalInfo?.professionalTitle || 'professional';\n  const skillsList = (ctx?.skills || []).map((s: { name?: string }) => s.name).filter(Boolean).join(', ') || '';\n  const yearsOfExperience = estimateYearsOfExperience(ctx?.workExperience || []);\n\n  // Tone modifier\n  const toneModifier = body.tone && TONE_MODIFIERS[body.tone]\n    ? `\\n\\n${TONE_MODIFIERS[body.tone]}`\n    : '';\n\n  let userMessage = '';\n  let systemPrompt = BASE_SYSTEM_PROMPT + toneModifier;\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Professional Summary\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  if (section === 'professionalSummary') {\n    switch (action) {\n      case 'generate':\n        userMessage = `Write a professional summary for a ${jobTitle} with ${yearsOfExperience} years of experience. Skills: ${skillsList}. Make it ATS-friendly, 2-3 sentences.`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'rewrite':\n        userMessage = `Rewrite this professional summary to be more impactful and ATS-optimized.\\n\\nCurrent summary:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'improve':\n        userMessage = `Improve this professional summary with stronger action verbs and quantifiable achievements.\\n\\nCurrent summary:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'shorten':\n        userMessage = `Shorten this professional summary to 1-2 sentences while keeping key information.\\n\\nCurrent summary:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'expand':\n        userMessage = `Expand this professional summary with more detail about achievements and expertise.\\n\\nCurrent summary:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'more_professional':\n        userMessage = `Make this professional summary more formal and executive-level.\\n\\nCurrent summary:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'more_technical':\n        userMessage = `Make this professional summary more technically focused, emphasizing tools, technologies, and methodologies.\\n\\nCurrent summary:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'simplify':\n        userMessage = `Simplify this professional summary for clarity, removing jargon.\\n\\nCurrent summary:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'grammar':\n        userMessage = `Check and fix any grammar, spelling, or writing issues in this professional summary.\\n\\nCurrent summary:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'keywords':\n        userMessage = `Suggest ATS-friendly keywords commonly found in job descriptions for a ${jobTitle}. Explain why each keyword is valuable. Format as: keyword - reason.`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'custom':\n        userMessage = `${customPrompt || ''}\\n\\n${content ? `Current summary:\\n${content}` : ''}`;\n        break;\n\n      default:\n        userMessage = `Improve this professional summary.\\n\\nCurrent summary:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Work Experience\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  else if (section === 'workExperience') {\n    const targetItem = findWorkExperienceItem(ctx, itemId);\n    const position = targetItem?.position || 'this role';\n    const company = targetItem?.company || 'the company';\n\n    switch (action) {\n      case 'generate':\n        userMessage = `Write 3-5 bullet points for a ${position} role at ${company}. Responsibilities: ${content || '(not provided)'}. Technologies: ${skillsList || 'from the skills section'}. Use strong action verbs and measurable achievements.`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'rewrite':\n        userMessage = `Rewrite these work experience bullet points to be more impactful.\\n\\nPosition: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'improve':\n        userMessage = `Improve these bullet points with stronger action verbs and measurable impact.\\n\\nPosition: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'expand':\n        userMessage = `Expand these bullet points with more detail and achievements.\\n\\nPosition: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'condense':\n        userMessage = `Condense these bullet points to be more concise while keeping key achievements.\\n\\nPosition: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'shorten':\n        userMessage = `Shorten these bullet points while retaining the most important information.\\n\\nPosition: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'achievement':\n        userMessage = `Transform these responsibilities into measurable achievements with quantified results. Add metrics, percentages, or numbers where possible.\\n\\nPosition: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'more_professional':\n        userMessage = `Make these work experience bullet points more formal and executive-level.\\n\\nPosition: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'more_technical':\n        userMessage = `Make these work experience bullet points more technically focused, emphasizing specific tools, technologies, and methodologies used.\\n\\nPosition: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'simplify':\n        userMessage = `Simplify these work experience bullet points for clarity, removing jargon.\\n\\nPosition: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'grammar':\n        userMessage = `Check and fix any grammar, spelling, or writing issues.\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'keywords':\n        userMessage = `Suggest ATS-friendly keywords commonly found in job descriptions for a ${position} role. Explain why each keyword is valuable. Format as: keyword - reason.`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'custom':\n        userMessage = `${customPrompt || ''}\\n\\n${content ? `Position: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content}` : ''}`;\n        break;\n\n      default:\n        userMessage = `Improve these work experience bullet points.\\n\\nPosition: ${position} at ${company}\\n\\nCurrent bullet points:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Education\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  else if (section === 'education') {\n    const targetItem = findEducationItem(ctx, itemId);\n    const degree = targetItem?.degree || 'this degree';\n    const field = targetItem?.field || '';\n    const institution = targetItem?.institution || 'this institution';\n\n    switch (action) {\n      case 'generate':\n        userMessage = `Write a brief description for a ${degree}${field ? ` in ${field}` : ''} from ${institution}. Highlight relevant coursework, honors, or activities if applicable.`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'improve':\n        userMessage = `Improve this education entry description.\\n\\nDegree: ${degree}${field ? ` in ${field}` : ''} at ${institution}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'rewrite':\n        userMessage = `Rewrite this education entry to be more impactful.\\n\\nDegree: ${degree}${field ? ` in ${field}` : ''} at ${institution}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'expand':\n        userMessage = `Expand this education entry with more detail about relevant coursework, projects, honors, or activities.\\n\\nDegree: ${degree}${field ? ` in ${field}` : ''} at ${institution}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'shorten':\n        userMessage = `Shorten this education entry while keeping the most relevant information.\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'grammar':\n        userMessage = `Check and fix any grammar, spelling, or writing issues in this education entry.\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'custom':\n        userMessage = `${customPrompt || ''}\\n\\n${content ? `Degree: ${degree}${field ? ` in ${field}` : ''} at ${institution}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content}` : ''}`;\n        break;\n\n      default:\n        userMessage = `Improve this education entry.\\n\\nDegree: ${degree}${field ? ` in ${field}` : ''} at ${institution}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Skills\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  else if (section === 'skills') {\n    switch (action) {\n      case 'skills_suggest':\n        userMessage = `Suggest 8-10 relevant skills for a ${jobTitle} with ${skillsList ? `current skills including: ${skillsList}` : 'no existing skills listed'}. Include both technical and soft skills. Return as a comma-separated list.`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'improve':\n        userMessage = `Improve and organize these skills to be more ATS-friendly and impactful.\\n\\nCurrent skills:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'rewrite':\n        userMessage = `Rewrite and reorganize these skills for better presentation and ATS optimization.\\n\\nCurrent skills:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'keywords':\n        userMessage = `Suggest ATS-friendly keywords commonly found in job descriptions for a ${jobTitle}. Explain why each keyword is valuable. Format as: keyword - reason.`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'grammar':\n        userMessage = `Check and fix any spelling, formatting, or consistency issues in this skills list.\\n\\nCurrent skills:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'custom':\n        userMessage = `${customPrompt || ''}\\n\\n${content ? `Current skills:\\n${content}` : ''}`;\n        break;\n\n      default:\n        userMessage = `Improve these skills.\\n\\nCurrent skills:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Projects\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  else if (section === 'projects') {\n    const targetItem = findProjectItem(ctx, itemId);\n    const projectName = targetItem?.name || 'this project';\n    const technologies = targetItem?.technologies?.join(', ') || '';\n\n    switch (action) {\n      case 'generate':\n        userMessage = `Write a description for a project called \"${projectName}\"${technologies ? ` using ${technologies}` : ''}. Make it concise, highlight the purpose, and emphasize measurable outcomes.`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'improve':\n        userMessage = `Improve this project description to be more impactful and results-oriented.\\n\\nProject: ${projectName}${technologies ? `\\nTechnologies: ${technologies}` : ''}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'rewrite':\n        userMessage = `Rewrite this project description for stronger impact.\\n\\nProject: ${projectName}${technologies ? `\\nTechnologies: ${technologies}` : ''}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'expand':\n        userMessage = `Expand this project description with more detail about the problem solved, approach taken, and results achieved.\\n\\nProject: ${projectName}${technologies ? `\\nTechnologies: ${technologies}` : ''}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'shorten':\n        userMessage = `Shorten this project description while keeping the most important information.\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'achievement':\n        userMessage = `Transform this project description to emphasize measurable achievements and quantified results.\\n\\nProject: ${projectName}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'grammar':\n        userMessage = `Check and fix any grammar, spelling, or writing issues in this project description.\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'custom':\n        userMessage = `${customPrompt || ''}\\n\\n${content ? `Project: ${projectName}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content}` : ''}`;\n        break;\n\n      default:\n        userMessage = `Improve this project description.\\n\\nProject: ${projectName}\\n\\nCurrent description:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Certifications\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  else if (section === 'certifications') {\n    switch (action) {\n      case 'improve':\n        userMessage = `Improve this certification entry for better presentation.\\n\\nCurrent entry:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'grammar':\n        userMessage = `Check and fix any grammar, spelling, or writing issues in this certification entry.\\n\\nCurrent entry:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'custom':\n        userMessage = `${customPrompt || ''}\\n\\n${content ? `Current entry:\\n${content}` : ''}`;\n        break;\n\n      default:\n        userMessage = `Improve this certification entry.\\n\\nCurrent entry:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Languages\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  else if (section === 'languages') {\n    switch (action) {\n      case 'improve':\n        userMessage = `Improve this language entry for better presentation.\\n\\nCurrent entry:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'grammar':\n        userMessage = `Check and fix any formatting or consistency issues in this language entry.\\n\\nCurrent entry:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n\n      case 'custom':\n        userMessage = `${customPrompt || ''}\\n\\n${content ? `Current entry:\\n${content}` : ''}`;\n        break;\n\n      default:\n        userMessage = `Improve this language entry.\\n\\nCurrent entry:\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Custom / Fallback\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  else {\n    if (action === 'custom') {\n      userMessage = `${customPrompt || ''}\\n\\n${content ? `Content:\\n${content}` : ''}`;\n    } else {\n      userMessage = `${action}: ${content || '(no content provided)'}`;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Override for grammar action on any section that wasn't handled specifically\n  if (action === 'grammar' && section !== 'professionalSummary' && section !== 'workExperience' && section !== 'education' && section !== 'skills' && section !== 'projects' && section !== 'certifications' && section !== 'languages') {\n    userMessage = `Check and fix grammar, spelling, repetition, weak wording, and passive voice. Return only the corrected text.\\n\\n${content || '(empty)'}`;\n  }\n\n  // Override for keywords action on any section that wasn't handled specifically\n  if (action === 'keywords' && section !== 'professionalSummary' && section !== 'workExperience' && section !== 'skills') {\n    userMessage = `Suggest ATS-friendly keywords commonly found in job descriptions for a ${jobTitle}. Explain why each keyword is valuable. Format as: keyword - reason.`;\n  }\n\n  return { systemPrompt, userMessage };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction estimateYearsOfExperience(workExperience?: ResumeContext['workExperience']): string {\n  if (!workExperience || workExperience.length === 0) return 'several';\n  let totalYears = 0;\n  for (const exp of workExperience) {\n    if (exp.startDate) {\n      const start = new Date(exp.startDate);\n      const end = exp.current ? new Date() : (exp.endDate ? new Date(exp.endDate) : new Date());\n      const diffMs = end.getTime() - start.getTime();\n      totalYears += Math.max(0, Math.round(diffMs / (365.25 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)));\n    }\n  }\n  return totalYears > 0 ? String(totalYears) : 'several';\n}\n\nfunction findWorkExperienceItem(\n  ctx: ResumeContext | undefined,\n  itemId?: string\n): ResumeContext['workExperience'] extends (infer T)[] | undefined ? NonNullable<T> : never {\n  if (!ctx?.workExperience || !itemId) return {} as never;\n  return ctx.workExperience.find((item) => item.id === itemId) || ({} as never);\n}\n\nfunction findEducationItem(\n  ctx: ResumeContext | undefined,\n  itemId?: string\n): ResumeContext['education'] extends (infer T)[] | undefined ? Authentication\n    const auth = await getAuthUser();\n    if (!auth) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { success: false, error: 'Unauthorized' },\n        { status: 401 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 2. Parse & validate request body\n    const body: AiAssistBody = await request.json();\n    const { section, action, content, customPrompt, tone, resumeContext, itemId, field } = body;\n\n    if (!section || !action) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { success: false, error: 'Missing required fields: section and action' },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (!VALID_SECTIONS.includes(section as Section)) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { success: false, error: `Invalid section. Must be one of: ${VALID_SECTIONS.join(', ')}` },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (!VALID_ACTIONS.includes(action as Action)) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { success: false, error: `Invalid action. Must be one of: ${VALID_ACTIONS.join(', ')}` },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (action === 'custom' && !customPrompt) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { success: false, error: 'customPrompt is required when action is \"custom\"' },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 3. Resolve resume ID from route params\n    const { id } = await params;\n\n    // 4. Ownership check\n    const resume = await db.resume.findUnique({\n      where: { id },\n    });\n\n    if (!resume || resume.user_id !== auth.userId) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { success: false, error: 'Resume not found' },\n        { status: 404 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 5. Feature gating\n    const usage = await checkAndIncrementUsage(auth.userId, FEATURE_KEYS.AI_RESUME_BUILDER);\n    if (!usage.allowed) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        {\n          success: false,\n          error: usage.reason || 'limit_reached',\n          limit: usage.limit,\n          used: usage.used,\n          planName: usage.planName,\n        },\n        { status: 403 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 6. Build the AI prompt\n    const { systemPrompt, userMessage } = buildPrompt(section, action, body);\n\n    // 7. Call the AI SDK\n    const zai = await ZAI.create();\n    const completion = await zai.chat.completions.create({\n      messages: [\n        { role: 'assistant', content: systemPrompt },\n        { role: 'user', content: userMessage },\n      ],\n      thinking: { type: 'disabled' },\n    });\n\n    const aiResponse = completion.choices[0]?.message?.content?.trim();\n\n    if (!aiResponse) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { success: false, error: 'Failed to generate suggestion' },\n        { status: 500 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 8. Save to AiHistory\n    await db.aiHistory.create({\n      data: {\n        resume_id: id,\n        user_id: auth.userId,\n        section,\n        action,\n        prompt: systemPrompt + '\\n\\n' + userMessage,\n        result: aiResponse,\n        original_text: content || '',\n      },\n    });\n\n    // 9. Return success\n    return NextResponse.json({\n      success: true,\n      result: aiResponse,\n      action,\n      section,\n      usage: {\n        limit: usage.limit,\n        used: usage.used + 1, // reflect the just-incremented count\n      },\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('[AI Assist] Error:', error);\n\n    // Handle ZAI-specific errors gracefully\n    if (error instanceof Error) {\n      const message = error.message.toLowerCase();\n      if (\n        message.includes('rate limit') ||\n        message.includes('quota') ||\n        message.includes('too many requests')\n      ) {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { success: false, error: 'AI service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again in a moment.' },\n          { status: 503 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      if (\n        message.includes('api key') ||\n        message.includes('authentication') ||\n        message.includes('unauthorized')\n      ) {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { success: false, error: 'AI service configuration error. Please contact support.' },\n          { status: 500 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      if (\n        message.includes('timeout') ||\n        message.includes('network') ||\n        message.includes('fetch')\n      ) {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { success: false, error: 'AI service request timed out. Please try again.' },\n          { status: 504 }\n        );\n      }\n    }\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { success: false, error: 'Failed to generate suggestion' },\n      { status: 500 {"version":3,"sources":["file:///home/z/my-project/src/lib/subscription/service.ts"],"sourcesContent":["import { db } from '@/lib/db';\nimport { FEATURE_KEYS, FEATURE_USAGE_MAP, type FeatureKey } from './features';\n\n/**\n * Get a user's effective plan (defaults to Free if no subscription exists).\n * Creates a default subscription + usage record on first access.\n */\nexport async function getUserPlan(userId: string) {\n  // Ensure user has a subscription (default to Free)\n  let subscription = await db.subscription.findUnique({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n\n  if (!subscription) {\n    const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n    if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found in database');\n\n    subscription = await db.subscription.create({\n      data: {\n        user_id: userId,\n        plan_id: freePlan.id,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      },\n      include: { plan: true },\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Ensure user has a usage record\n  const existingUsage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n  if (!existingUsage) {\n    await db.userUsage.create({ data: { user_id: userId } });\n  }\n\n  return subscription;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get plan features for a specific plan ID.\n */\nexport async function getPlanFeatures(planId: string) {\n  return db.planFeature.findMany({\n    where: { plan_id: planId },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a user can access a specific feature.\n * Returns { allowed: boolean, reason?: string, limit?: number, used?: number }\n */\nexport async function canAccess(\n  userId: string,\n  featureKey: FeatureKey\n): Promise<{\n  allowed: boolean;\n  reason?: string;\n  limit: number;\n  used: number;\n  planName: string;\n}> {\n  const subscription = await getUserPlan(userId);\n  const features = await getPlanFeatures(subscription.plan_id);\n\n  const feature = features.find(f => f.feature_key === featureKey);\n\n  // Feature not configured for this plan = not allowed\n  if (!feature || !feature.enabled) {\n    return {\n      allowed: false,\n      reason: subscription.plan.name === 'free' ? 'upgrade_required' : 'feature_not_available',\n      limit: 0,\n      used: 0,\n      planName: subscription.plan.name,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Unlimited access\n  if (feature.limit_value === -1) {\n    return {\n      allowed: true,\n      limit: -1,\n      used: 0,\n      planName: subscription.plan.name,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Check usage limit\n  const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[featureKey];\n  let used = 0;\n\n  if (usageField) {\n    const usage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n    if (usage) {\n      used = usage[usageField] ?? 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const allowed = used < feature.limit_value;\n\n  return {\n    allowed,\n    reason: allowed ? undefined : 'limit_reached',\n    limit: feature.limit_value,\n    used,\n    planName: subscription.plan.name,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a user can use a feature and optionally increment usage.\n * Returns the access result, and optionally increments the usage counter.\n */\nexport async function checkAndIncrementUsage(\n  userId: string,\n  featureKey: FeatureKey,\n  increment = true\n) {\n  const access = await canAccess(userId, featureKey);\n\n  if (!access.allowed) return access;\n\n  if (increment) {\n    const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[featureKey];\n    if (usageField) {\n      await db.userUsage.update({\n        where: { user_id: userId },\n        data: { [usageField]: { increment: 1 } },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return access;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get current usage for a user.\n */\nexport async function getUserUsage(userId: string) {\n  const subscription = await getUserPlan(userId);\n  const features = await getPlanFeatures(subscription.plan_id);\n  const usage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n\n  const featureUsage: Record<string, { limit: number; used: number; enabled: boolean }> = {};\n\n  for (const feature of features) {\n    const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[feature.feature_key];\n    const used = usage && usageField ? (usage[usageField] ?? 0) : 0;\n\n    featureUsage[feature.feature_key] = {\n      limit: feature.limit_value,\n      used,\n      enabled: feature.enabled,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    plan: {\n      id: subscription.plan.id,\n      name: subscription.plan.name,\n      display_name: subscription.plan.display_name,\n    },\n    subscription: {\n      status: subscription.status,\n      started_at: subscription.started_at,\n      expires_at: subscription.expires_at,\n      billing_cycle: subscription.billing_cycle,\n    },\n    usage: featureUsage,\n    raw: usage ?? {\n      resume_count: 0,\n      cover_letter_count: 0,\n      ai_requests: 0,\n      resume_reviews: 0,\n      exports_count: 0,\n      ats_checks: 0,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Get all active plans with their features.\n */\nexport async function getPlans() {\n  return db.plan.findMany({\n    where: { is_active: true },\n    include: { plan_features: true },\n    orderBy: { sort_order: 'asc' },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Upgrade a user's subscription to a new plan.\n */\nexport async function upgradeSubscription(\n  userId: string,\n  newPlanId: string,\n  billingCycle: 'monthly' | 'yearly' = 'monthly'\n) {\n  const newPlan = await db.plan.findUnique({ where: { id: newPlanId } });\n  if (!newPlan) throw new Error('Plan not found');\n\n  const price = billingCycle === 'yearly' ? newPlan.price_yearly : newPlan.price_monthly;\n  const now = new Date();\n  const periodEnd = new Date(now);\n  if (billingCycle === 'yearly') {\n    periodEnd.setFullYear(periodEnd.getFullYear() + 1);\n  } else {\n    periodEnd.setMonth(periodEnd.getMonth() + 1);\n  }\n\n  const existing = await db.subscription.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n\n  if (existing) {\n    return db.subscription.update({\n      where: { user_id: userId },\n      data: {\n        plan_id: newPlanId,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: billingCycle,\n        current_period_start: now,\n        current_period_end: periodEnd,\n        expires_at: periodEnd,\n        cancelled_at: null,\n        updated_at: now,\n      },\n      include: { plan: true },\n    });\n  }\n\n  return db.subscription.create({\n    data: {\n      user_id: userId,\n      plan_id: newPlanId,\n      status: 'active',\n      billing_cycle: billingCycle,\n      current_period_start: now,\n      current_period_end: periodEnd,\n      expires_at: periodEnd,\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Cancel a user's subscription (keep active until end of billing period).\n */\nexport async function cancelSubscription(userId: string) {\n  const subscription = await db.subscription.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n  if (!subscription) throw new Error('No subscription found');\n\n  if (subscription.plan.name === 'free') {\n    throw new Error('Cannot cancel free plan');\n  }\n\n  return db.subscription.update({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    data: {\n      status: 'cancelled',\n      cancelled_at: new Date(),\n      // Keep access until end of current period\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Downgrade a user to the free plan.\n */\nexport async function downgradeToFree(userId: string) {\n  const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n  if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found');\n\n  return db.subscription.update({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    data: {\n      plan_id: freePlan.id,\n      status: 'active',\n      billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      current_period_start: new Date(),\n      current_period_end: null,\n      expires_at: null,\n      cancelled_at: null,\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Initialize usage for a new user (called after signup).\n */\nexport async function initializeUsage(userId: string) {\n  const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n  if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found');\n\n  await db.$transaction([\n    db.subscription.create({\n      data: {\n        user_id: userId,\n        plan_id: freePlan.id,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      },\n    }),\n    db.userUsage.create({\n      data: { user_id: userId },\n    }),\n  ]);\n}\n\n// Quick helper: is user on free plan?\nexport async function return { score: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score)), suggestions };\n}\n\nfunction analyzeFormatting(resume: ResumeData): { score: number; issues: FormattingIssue[] } {\n  let score = 100;\n  const issues: FormattingIssue[] = [];\n\n  // Check professional summary\n  if (!resume.professionalSummary || resume.professionalSummary.trim().length === 0) {\n    score -= 20;\n    issues.push({\n      issue: 'Missing professional summary',\n      suggestion: 'Add a 2-3 sentence professional summary highlighting your key achievements and target role',\n      severity: 'error',\n    });\n  } else if score -= 15;\n      issues.push({\n        issue: 'Work experience entries lack descriptions',\n        suggestion: 'Add bullet-point descriptions for each role highlighting responsibilities and resume.workExperience.length > 0) {\n    const datePattern = /^(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|oct|nov|dec|january|february|march|april|may|june|july|august|september|october|november|december)\\s*\\d{4}$/i;\n    const datePattern2 = score -= 3;\n          issues.push({\n            issue: `Inconsistent date format at \"${exp.position}\" at ${exp.company}`,\n            suggestion: 'Use a consistent date format like \"Jan 2023\" issues };\n}\n\nfunction analyzeWorkExperience(workExperience: WorkExperience[]): { score: number; suggestions: Suggestion[] } {\n  if (!workExperience || workExperience.length === 0) {\n    return {\n      score: 0,\n      suggestions: [\n        {\n          category: 'workExperience',\n          message: 'Add work experience to significantly improve your resume score',\n          priority: 'high',\n          section: 'workExperience',\n        },\n      ],\n    };\n  }\n\n  let score = 0;\n  const suggestions: Suggestion[] = [];\n\n  // Number of entries scoring\n  const count = workExperience.length;\n  if (count === 1) score = 40;\n  else if (count === 2) score = 70;\n  else if (count >= 3) score = 90;\n\n  // Average description length\n  const entriesWithDesc = workExperience.filter((e) => e.description && e.description.trim().length > 0);\n  if (entriesWithDesc.length > 0) {\n    const avgDescLen = entriesWithDesc.reduce((sum, e) => sum + e.description.trim().length, 0) / entriesWithDesc.length;\n\n    if (avgDescLen < 50) {\n      score -= 20;\n      suggestions.push({\n        category: 'workExperience',\n        message: 'Work experience descriptions are too brief — add more detail about your responsibilities and achievements',\n        priority: 'high',\n        section: 'workExperience',\n      });\n    } else if (avgDescLen < 150) {\n      score -= 10;\n      suggestions.push({\n        category: 'workExperience',\n        message: 'Expand your work experience descriptions with more specific details and accomplishments',\n        priority: 'medium',\n        section: 'workExperience',\n      });\n    } else if (avgDescLen >= 150) {\n      score += 5; // Bonus for detailed descriptions\n    }\n  } else {\n    score -= 20;\n    suggestions.push({\n      category: 'workExperience',\n      message: 'Add descriptions to your work experience entries with bullet points',\n      priority: 'high',\n      section: 'workExperience',\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Current job flag\n  const hasCurrentJob = workExperience.some((e) => e.currentJob);\n  if (hasCurrentJob) {\n    score += 5;\n  }\n\n  // Action verbs check\n  let totalBullets = 0;\n  let actionVerbBullets = 0;\n\n  for (const exp of entriesWithDesc) {\n    const bullets = exp.description\n      .split('\\n')\n      .map((b) => b.trim())\n      .filter((b) => b.length > 0 && (b.startsWith('-') || b.startsWith('•') || b.startsWith('*')));\n\n    for (const bullet of bullets) {\n      const cleanBullet = bullet.replace(/^[-•*]\\s*/, '').trim();\n      if (cleanBullet.length === 0) continue;\n      totalBullets++;\n      const firstWord = cleanBullet.split(/\\s+/)[0];\n      if (ACTION_VERBS.some((verb) => firstWord.toLowerCase() === verb.toLowerCase())) {\n        actionVerbBullets++;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (totalBullets > 0) {\n    const actionVerbRatio = actionVerbBullets / totalBullets;\n    if (actionVerbRatio < 0.3) {\n      score -= 10;\n      suggestions.push({\n        category: 'workExperience',\n        message: 'Start more bullet points with strong action verbs (Led, Developed, Achieved, Implemented, etc.)',\n        priority: 'medium',\n        section: 'workExperience',\n      });\n    } else if (actionVerbRatio >= 0.6) {\n      score += 5; // Bonus for strong action verbs\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { score: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score)), suggestions };\n}\n\nfunction analyzeEducation(education: Education[]): { score: number; suggestions: Suggestion[] } {\n  if (!education || education.length === 0) {\n    return {\n      score: 0,\n      suggestions: [\n        {\n          category: 'education',\n          message: 'Add education information to your resume',\n          priority: 'high',\n          section: 'education',\n        },\n      ],\n    };\n  }\n\n  let score = education.length === 1 ? 60 : 85;\n  const suggestions: Suggestion[] = [];\n\n  // Check degree and field\n  const hasDegree = education.some((e) => e.degree && e.degree.trim().length > 0);\n  const hasField = education.some((e) => e.fieldOfStudy && e.fieldOfStudy.trim().length > 0);\n\n  if (!hasDegree) {\n    score -= 20;\n    suggestions.push({\n      category: 'education',\n      message: 'Specify the degree type for your education entries (e.g., B.Sc., M.A., MBA)',\n      priority: 'high',\n      section: 'education',\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (!hasField) {\n    score -= 15;\n    suggestions.push({\n      category: 'education',\n      message: 'Add your field of study to strengthen your educational background',\n      priority: 'medium',\n      section: 'education',\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Check dates\n  const hasDates = education.some((e) => e.startDate && e.startDate.trim().length > 0);\n  if (!hasDates) {\n    score -= 10;\n    suggestions.push({\n      category: 'education',\n      message: 'Add graduation or attendance dates for education entries',\n      priority: 'low',\n      section: 'education',\n    });\n  }\n\n  return { score: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score)), suggestions };\n}\n\nfunction analyzeSkills(skills: Skill[]): { score: number; suggestions: Suggestion[] } {\n  if (!skills || skills.length === 0) {\n    return {\n      score: 0,\n      suggestions: [\n        {\n          category: 'skills',\n          message: 'Add skills to help ATS systems match your resume to job requirements',\n          priority: 'high',\n          section: 'skills',\n        },\n      ],\n    };\n  }\n\n  let score: number;\n  const suggestions: Suggestion[] = [];\n  const count = skills.length;\n\n  if (count <= 3) score = 30;\n  else if (count <= 6) score = 60;\n  else if (count <= 10) score = 80;\n  else score = 95;\n\n  if (count < 4) {\n    suggestions.push({\n      category: 'skills',\n      message: 'Add more skills — aim for at least 7-10 for better ATS matching',\n      priority: 'high',\n      section: 'skills',\n    });\n  } else if (count < 7) {\n    suggestions.push({\n      category: 'skills',\n      message: 'Consider adding more relevant skills to improve keyword matching',\n      priority: 'medium',\n      section: 'skills',\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Check skill name length (very short names may be too vague)\n  const shortSkills = skills.filter((s) => s.name.trim().split(/\\s+/).length < 2).length;\n  const shortRatio = shortSkills / count;\n  if (shortRatio > 0.8 && count > 3) {\n    score -= 5;\n    suggestions.push({\n      category: 'skills',\n      message: 'Include more specific skill names (e.g., \"React.js\" instead of just \"React\")',\n      priority: 'low',\n      section: 'skills',\n    });\n  }\n\n  return { score: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score)), suggestions };\n}\n\nfunction analyzeReadability(resume: ResumeData): number {\n  // Total word count across all text sections\n  const texts: string[] = [];\n\n  if (resume.professionalSummary) texts.push(resume.professionalSummary);\n  if (resume.personalInfo?.professionalTitle) texts.push(resume.personalInfo.professionalTitle);\n\n  if (resume.workExperience) {\n    for (const exp of resume.workExperience) {\n      if (exp.description) texts.push(exp.description);\n      if (exp.position) texts.push(exp.position);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (resume.education) {\n    for (const edu of resume.education) {\n      if (edu.degree) texts.push(edu.degree);\n      if (edu.fieldOfStudy) texts.push(edu.fieldOfStudy);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (resume.projects) {\n    for (const proj of resume.projects) {\n      if (proj.description) texts.push(proj.description);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (resume.additionalInfo?.text) texts.push(resume.additionalInfo.text);\n\n  const totalWords = texts.reduce((sum, text) => sum + wordCount(text), 0);\n\n  // Ideal range: 300-800 words\n  if (totalWords < 100) return 20;\n  if (totalWords < 200) return 45;\n  if (totalWords < 300) return 65;\n  if (totalWords <= 800) return 90;\n  if (totalWords <= 1000) return 80;\n  if (totalWords <= 1200) return 65;\n  return 45; // Way too long\n}\n\nfunction analyzeAchievements(workExperience: WorkExperience[]): { score: number; suggestions: Suggestion[] } {\n  if (!workExperience || workExperience.length === 0) {\n    return { score: 0, suggestions: [] };\n  }\n\n  const suggestions: Suggestion[] = [];\n  let totalBullets = 0;\n  let quantifiableBullets = 0;\n\n  const metricsPattern = /\\d+%|\\$[\\d,.]+|\\d+\\+?\\s*(?:users?|clients?|customers?|members?|people|projects?|teams?|reports?|sites?|pages?|articles?|products?|services?|applications?|systems?|processes?|campaigns?|accounts?)/i;\n  const numberPattern = /\\b\\d{2,}\\b/;\n\n  for (const exp of workExperience) {\n    if (!exp.description) continue;\n    const bullets = exp.description\n      .split('\\n')\n      .map((b) => b.trim())\n      .filter((b) => b.length > 0);\n\n    for (const bullet of bullets) {\n      const clean = bullet.replace(/^[-•*]\\s*/, '').trim();\n      if (clean.length === 0) continue;\n      totalBullets++;\n\n      if (metricsPattern.test(clean) || numberPattern.test(clean)) {\n        quantifiableBullets++;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (totalBullets === 0) return { score: 10, suggestions };\n\n  const ratio = quantifiableBullets / totalBullets;\n  let score: number;\n\n  if (ratio >= 0.6) score = 95;\n  else if (ratio >= 0.4) score = 80;\n  else if (ratio >= 0.2) score = 60;\n  else if (ratio >= 0.1) score = 40;\n  else score = 20;\n\n  if (ratio < 0.3) {\n    suggestions.push({\n      category: 'achievements',\n      message: 'Add quantifiable achievements with numbers, percentages, or dollar amounts to your bullet points',\n      priority: 'high',\n      section: 'workExperience',\n    });\n  } else if (ratio < 0.5) {\n    suggestions.push({\n      category: 'achievements',\n      message: 'More of your bullet points should include quantifiable results (e.g., \"Increased sales by 25%\")',\n      priority: 'medium',\n      section: 'workExperience',\n    });\n  }\n\n  return { score, suggestions };\n}\n\n// ─── AI Analysis ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nasync function analyzeWithAI(\n  resume: ResumeData,\n  jobDescription?: string\n): Promise<{\n  keywordAnalysis: KeywordAnalysis;\n  grammarIssues: GrammarIssue[];\n  grammarScore: number;\n  keywordScore: number;\n  skillsMatchScore: number;\n  aiSuggestions: Suggestion[];\n}> {\n  const zai = await ZAI.create();\n\n  const resumeText = `\nName: ${resume.personalInfo?.fullName || 'N/A'}\nTitle: ${resume.personalInfo?.professionalTitle || 'N/A'}\nSummary: ${resume.professionalSummary || 'None'}\nSkills: ${(resume.skills || []).map((s) => s.name).join(', ') || 'None'}\nWork 'Present' : e.endDate}): ${e.description || 'No description'}`\n  )\n  .join('\\n')}\nEducation:\n${(resume.education || [])\n  .map((e) => `- ${e.degree} in ${e.fieldOfStudy || 'N/A'} from ${e.school} (${e.startDate} - ${e.endDate})`)\n  .join('\\n')}\nProjects:\n${(resume.projects || [])\n  .map((p) => `- ${p.name}: ${p.description || 'No description'} (Tech: ${p.technologies || 'N/A'})`)\n  .join('\\n')}\n  `.trim();\n\n  let prompt: string;\n\n  if (jobDescription && jobDescription.trim()) {\n    prompt = `Analyze this resume against the following job description and respond ONLY with valid JSON, no markdown.\n\nJOB DESCRIPTION:\n${jobDescription}\n\nRESUME:\n${resumeText}\n\nRespond with this exact JSON structure:\n{\n  \"keywordAnalysis\": {\n    \"matched\": [\"keyword1\", \"keyword2\"],\n    \"missing\": [\"keyword1\", \"keyword2\"],\n    \"suggested\": [\"keyword1\", \"keyword2\"],\n    \"matchPercentage\": 65\n  },\n  \"grammarIssues\": [\n    { \"message\": \"description of issue\", \"suggestion\": \"how to fix it\", \"severity\": \"warning\" }\n  ],\n  \"grammarScore\": 85,\n  \"keywordScore\": 70,\n  \"skillsMatchScore\": 65,\n  \"suggestions\": [\n    { \"category\": \"keywords\", \"message\": \"actionable suggestion\", \"priority\": \"high\", \"section\": \"skills\" }\n  ]\n}\n\nInstructions:\n- \"matched\": skills/keywords from the job description found in the resume (case-insensitive)\n- \"missing\": important skills/keywords from the job description NOT found in the resume\n- \"suggested\": additional relevant keywords that could strengthen alignment\n- \"matchPercentage\": percentage of critical job keywords found in resume (0-100)\n- \"grammarIssues\": any grammar, spelling, or readability issues found in the resume text\n- \"grammarScore\": overall grammar and language quality (0-100)\n- \"keywordScore\": how well the resume's keywords align with the job (0-100)\n- \"skillsMatchScore\": how well listed skills match job requirements (0-100)\n- \"suggestions\": 3-5 actionable improvement suggestions\n- Only return the JSON, no explanation text`;\n  } else {\n    prompt = `Analyze this resume for ATS optimization and respond ONLY with valid JSON, no markdown.\n\nRESUME:\n${resumeText}\n\nRespond with this exact JSON structure:\n{\n  \"keywordAnalysis\": {\n    \"matched\": [\"existing keyword 1\", \"existing keyword 2\"],\n    \"missing\": [],\n    \"suggested\": [\"suggested keyword 1\", \"suggested keyword 2\", \"suggested keyword 3\"],\n    \"matchPercentage\": 70\n  },\n  \"grammarIssues\": [\n    { \"message\": \"description of issue\", \"suggestion\": \"how to fix it\", \"severity\": \"warning\" }\n  ],\n  \"grammarScore\": 85,\n  \"keywordScore\": 70,\n  \"skillsMatchScore\": 70,\n  \"suggestions\": [\n    { \"category\": \"keywords\", \"message\": \"actionable suggestion\", \"priority\": \"high\", \"section\": \"skills\" }\n  ]\n}\n\nInstructions:\n- \"matched\": strong, relevant keywords already present in the resume\n- \"missing\": empty array (no job description to compare against)\n- \"suggested\": 5-10 industry-relevant keywords/skills the resume should include based on the target role/industry\n- \"matchPercentage\": estimated ATS keyword strength (0-100)\n- \"grammarIssues\": any grammar, spelling, readability, or weak verb usage Authentication\n    const user = await getAuthUser(request);\n    if (!user) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { success: false, error: 'Unauthorized' },\n        { status: 401 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 3. Feature access check\n    const access = await checkAndIncrementUsage(user.userId, FEATURE_KEYS.ATS_CHECKER);\n    if (!access.allowed) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        {\n          success: false,\n          error: 'limit_reached',\n          limit: access.limit,\n          used: access.used,\n          planName: access.planName,\n        },\n        { status: 403 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    // 4. Merge all suggestions\n    const allSuggestions: Suggestion[] = [\n      ...contactResult.suggestions,\n      ...workResult.suggestions,\n      ...educationResult.suggestions,\n      ...skillsResult.suggestions,\n      ...achievementsResult.suggestions,\n      ...aiResult.aiSuggestions,\n    ];\n\n    // Sort by priority\n    const priorityOrder: Record<string, number> = { high: 0, medium: 1, low: 2 };\n    allSuggestions.sort((a, b) => (priorityOrder[a.priority] ?? 2) - (priorityOrder[b.priority] ?? 2));\n\n    // 10. Build formatted response\n    const categoryScoresFormatted: Record<string, CategoryScore> = {\n      contactInfo: { score: categoryScores.contactInfo, label: 'Contact Information' },\n      formatting: { score: categoryScores.formatting, label: 'Formatting' },\n      keywords: { score: categoryScores.keywords, label: 'Keywords' },\n      skillsMatch: { score: categoryScores.skillsMatch, label: 'Skills Match' },\n      workExperience: { score: categoryScores.workExperience, label: 'Work Experience' },\n      education: { score: categoryScores.education, label: 'Education' },\n      grammar: { score: categoryScores.grammar, label: 'Grammar' },\n      readability: { score: categoryScores.readability, label: 'Readability' },\n      achievements: { score: categoryScores.achievements, label: 'Achievements' },\n    };\n\n    // 11. /home/z/my-project/.next/dev/server/chunks/[root-of-the-server]__24608ebc._.js.map:7:    {"offset": {"line": 183, "column": 0}, "map": {"version":3,"sources":["file:///home/z/my-project/src/lib/subscription/service.ts"],"sourcesContent":["import { db } from '@/lib/db';\nimport { FEATURE_KEYS, FEATURE_USAGE_MAP, type FeatureKey } from './features';\n\n/**\n * Get a user's effective plan (defaults to Free if no subscription exists).\n * Creates a default subscription + usage record on first access.\n */\nexport async function getUserPlan(userId: string) {\n  // Ensure user has a subscription (default to Free)\n  let subscription = await db.subscription.findUnique({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n\n  if (!subscription) {\n    const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n    if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found in database');\n\n    subscription = await db.subscription.create({\n      data: {\n        user_id: userId,\n        plan_id: freePlan.id,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      },\n      include: { plan: true },\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Ensure user has a usage record\n  const existingUsage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n  if (!existingUsage) {\n    await db.userUsage.create({ data: { user_id: userId } });\n  }\n\n  return subscription;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get plan features for a specific plan ID.\n */\nexport async function getPlanFeatures(planId: string) {\n  return db.planFeature.findMany({\n    where: { plan_id: planId },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a user can access a specific feature.\n * Returns { allowed: boolean, reason?: string, limit?: number, used?: number }\n */\nexport async function canAccess(\n  userId: string,\n  featureKey: FeatureKey\n): Promise<{\n  allowed: boolean;\n  reason?: string;\n  limit: number;\n  used: number;\n  planName: string;\n}> {\n  const subscription = await getUserPlan(userId);\n  const features = await getPlanFeatures(subscription.plan_id);\n\n  const feature = features.find(f => f.feature_key === featureKey);\n\n  // Feature not configured for this plan = not allowed\n  if (!feature || !feature.enabled) {\n    return {\n      allowed: false,\n      reason: subscription.plan.name === 'free' ? 'upgrade_required' : 'feature_not_available',\n      limit: 0,\n      used: 0,\n      planName: subscription.plan.name,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Unlimited access\n  if (feature.limit_value === -1) {\n    return {\n      allowed: true,\n      limit: -1,\n      used: 0,\n      planName: subscription.plan.name,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Check usage limit\n  const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[featureKey];\n  let used = 0;\n\n  if (usageField) {\n    const usage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n    if (usage) {\n      used = usage[usageField] ?? 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const allowed = used < feature.limit_value;\n\n  return {\n    allowed,\n    reason: allowed ? undefined : 'limit_reached',\n    limit: feature.limit_value,\n    used,\n    planName: subscription.plan.name,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a user can use a feature and optionally increment usage.\n * Returns the access result, and optionally increments the usage counter.\n */\nexport async function checkAndIncrementUsage(\n  userId: string,\n  featureKey: FeatureKey,\n  increment = true\n) {\n  const access = await canAccess(userId, featureKey);\n\n  if (!access.allowed) return access;\n\n  if (increment) {\n    const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[featureKey];\n    if (usageField) {\n      await db.userUsage.update({\n        where: { user_id: userId },\n        data: { [usageField]: { increment: 1 } },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return access;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get current usage for a user.\n */\nexport async function getUserUsage(userId: string) {\n  const subscription = await getUserPlan(userId);\n  const features = await getPlanFeatures(subscription.plan_id);\n  const usage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n\n  const featureUsage: Record<string, { limit: number; used: number; enabled: boolean }> = {};\n\n  for (const feature of features) {\n    const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[feature.feature_key];\n    const used = usage && usageField ? (usage[usageField] ?? 0) : 0;\n\n    featureUsage[feature.feature_key] = {\n      limit: feature.limit_value,\n      used,\n      enabled: feature.enabled,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    plan: {\n      id: subscription.plan.id,\n      name: subscription.plan.name,\n      display_name: subscription.plan.display_name,\n    },\n    subscription: {\n      status: subscription.status,\n      started_at: subscription.started_at,\n      expires_at: subscription.expires_at,\n      billing_cycle: subscription.billing_cycle,\n    },\n    usage: featureUsage,\n    raw: usage ?? {\n      resume_count: 0,\n      cover_letter_count: 0,\n      ai_requests: 0,\n      resume_reviews: 0,\n      exports_count: 0,\n      ats_checks: 0,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Get all active plans with their features.\n */\nexport async function getPlans() {\n  return db.plan.findMany({\n    where: { is_active: true },\n    include: { plan_features: true },\n    orderBy: { sort_order: 'asc' },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Upgrade a user's subscription to a new plan.\n */\nexport async function upgradeSubscription(\n  userId: string,\n  newPlanId: string,\n  billingCycle: 'monthly' | 'yearly' = 'monthly'\n) {\n  const newPlan = await db.plan.findUnique({ where: { id: newPlanId } });\n  if (!newPlan) throw new Error('Plan not found');\n\n  const price = billingCycle === 'yearly' ? newPlan.price_yearly : newPlan.price_monthly;\n  const now = new Date();\n  const periodEnd = new Date(now);\n  if (billingCycle === 'yearly') {\n    periodEnd.setFullYear(periodEnd.getFullYear() + 1);\n  } else {\n    periodEnd.setMonth(periodEnd.getMonth() + 1);\n  }\n\n  const existing = await db.subscription.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n\n  if (existing) {\n    return db.subscription.update({\n      where: { user_id: userId },\n      data: {\n        plan_id: newPlanId,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: billingCycle,\n        current_period_start: now,\n        current_period_end: periodEnd,\n        expires_at: periodEnd,\n        cancelled_at: null,\n        updated_at: now,\n      },\n      include: { plan: true },\n    });\n  }\n\n  return db.subscription.create({\n    data: {\n      user_id: userId,\n      plan_id: newPlanId,\n      status: 'active',\n      billing_cycle: billingCycle,\n      current_period_start: now,\n      current_period_end: periodEnd,\n      expires_at: periodEnd,\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Cancel a user's subscription (keep active until end of billing period).\n */\nexport async function cancelSubscription(userId: string) {\n  const subscription = await db.subscription.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n  if (!subscription) throw new Error('No subscription found');\n\n  if (subscription.plan.name === 'free') {\n    throw new Error('Cannot cancel free plan');\n  }\n\n  return db.subscription.update({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    data: {\n      status: 'cancelled',\n      cancelled_at: new Date(),\n      // Keep access until end of current period\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Downgrade a user to the free plan.\n */\nexport async function downgradeToFree(userId: string) {\n  const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n  if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found');\n\n  return db.subscription.update({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    data: {\n      plan_id: freePlan.id,\n      status: 'active',\n      billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      current_period_start: new Date(),\n      current_period_end: null,\n      expires_at: null,\n      cancelled_at: null,\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Initialize usage for a new user (called after signup).\n */\nexport async function initializeUsage(userId: string) {\n  const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n  if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found');\n\n  await db.$transaction([\n    db.subscription.create({\n      data: {\n        user_id: userId,\n        plan_id: freePlan.id,\n        status: 'active',\n {"version":3,"sources":["file:///home/z/my-project/src/lib/subscription/service.ts"],"sourcesContent":["import { db } from '@/lib/db';\nimport { FEATURE_KEYS, FEATURE_USAGE_MAP, type FeatureKey } from './features';\n\n/**\n * Get a user's effective plan (defaults to Free if no subscription exists).\n * Creates a default subscription + usage record on first access.\n */\nexport async function getUserPlan(userId: string) {\n  // Ensure user has a subscription (default to Free)\n  let subscription = await db.subscription.findUnique({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n\n  if (!subscription) {\n    const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n    if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found in database');\n\n    subscription = await db.subscription.create({\n      data: {\n        user_id: userId,\n        plan_id: freePlan.id,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      },\n      include: { plan: true },\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Ensure user has a usage record\n  const existingUsage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n  if (!existingUsage) {\n    await db.userUsage.create({ data: { user_id: userId } });\n  }\n\n  return subscription;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get plan features for a specific plan ID.\n */\nexport async function getPlanFeatures(planId: string) {\n  return db.planFeature.findMany({\n    where: { plan_id: planId },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a user can access a specific feature.\n * Returns { allowed: boolean, reason?: string, limit?: number, used?: number }\n */\nexport async function canAccess(\n  userId: string,\n  featureKey: FeatureKey\n): Promise<{\n  allowed: boolean;\n  reason?: string;\n  limit: number;\n  used: number;\n  planName: string;\n}> {\n  const subscription = await getUserPlan(userId);\n  const features = await getPlanFeatures(subscription.plan_id);\n\n  const feature = features.find(f => f.feature_key === featureKey);\n\n  // Feature not configured for this plan = not allowed\n  if (!feature || !feature.enabled) {\n    return {\n      allowed: false,\n      reason: subscription.plan.name === 'free' ? 'upgrade_required' : 'feature_not_available',\n      limit: 0,\n      used: 0,\n      planName: subscription.plan.name,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Unlimited access\n  if (feature.limit_value === -1) {\n    return {\n      allowed: true,\n      limit: -1,\n      used: 0,\n      planName: subscription.plan.name,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Check usage limit\n  const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[featureKey];\n  let used = 0;\n\n  if (usageField) {\n    const usage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n    if (usage) {\n      used = usage[usageField] ?? 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const allowed = used < feature.limit_value;\n\n  return {\n    allowed,\n    reason: allowed ? undefined : 'limit_reached',\n    limit: feature.limit_value,\n    used,\n    planName: subscription.plan.name,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a user can use a feature and optionally increment usage.\n * Returns the access result, and optionally increments the usage counter.\n */\nexport async function checkAndIncrementUsage(\n  userId: string,\n  featureKey: FeatureKey,\n  increment = true\n) {\n  const access = await canAccess(userId, featureKey);\n\n  if (!access.allowed) return access;\n\n  if (increment) {\n    const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[featureKey];\n    if (usageField) {\n      await db.userUsage.update({\n        where: { user_id: userId },\n        data: { [usageField]: { increment: 1 } },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return access;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get current usage for a user.\n */\nexport async function getUserUsage(userId: string) {\n  const subscription = await getUserPlan(userId);\n  const features = await getPlanFeatures(subscription.plan_id);\n  const usage = await db.userUsage.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n\n  const featureUsage: Record<string, { limit: number; used: number; enabled: boolean }> = {};\n\n  for (const feature of features) {\n    const usageField = FEATURE_USAGE_MAP[feature.feature_key];\n    const used = usage && usageField ? (usage[usageField] ?? 0) : 0;\n\n    featureUsage[feature.feature_key] = {\n      limit: feature.limit_value,\n      used,\n      enabled: feature.enabled,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    plan: {\n      id: subscription.plan.id,\n      name: subscription.plan.name,\n      display_name: subscription.plan.display_name,\n    },\n    subscription: {\n      status: subscription.status,\n      started_at: subscription.started_at,\n      expires_at: subscription.expires_at,\n      billing_cycle: subscription.billing_cycle,\n    },\n    usage: featureUsage,\n    raw: usage ?? {\n      resume_count: 0,\n      cover_letter_count: 0,\n      ai_requests: 0,\n      resume_reviews: 0,\n      exports_count: 0,\n      ats_checks: 0,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Get all active plans with their features.\n */\nexport async function getPlans() {\n  return db.plan.findMany({\n    where: { is_active: true },\n    include: { plan_features: true },\n    orderBy: { sort_order: 'asc' },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Upgrade a user's subscription to a new plan.\n */\nexport async function upgradeSubscription(\n  userId: string,\n  newPlanId: string,\n  billingCycle: 'monthly' | 'yearly' = 'monthly'\n) {\n  const newPlan = await db.plan.findUnique({ where: { id: newPlanId } });\n  if (!newPlan) throw new Error('Plan not found');\n\n  const price = billingCycle === 'yearly' ? newPlan.price_yearly : newPlan.price_monthly;\n  const now = new Date();\n  const periodEnd = new Date(now);\n  if (billingCycle === 'yearly') {\n    periodEnd.setFullYear(periodEnd.getFullYear() + 1);\n  } else {\n    periodEnd.setMonth(periodEnd.getMonth() + 1);\n  }\n\n  const existing = await db.subscription.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n\n  if (existing) {\n    return db.subscription.update({\n      where: { user_id: userId },\n      data: {\n        plan_id: newPlanId,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: billingCycle,\n        current_period_start: now,\n        current_period_end: periodEnd,\n        expires_at: periodEnd,\n        cancelled_at: null,\n        updated_at: now,\n      },\n      include: { plan: true },\n    });\n  }\n\n  return db.subscription.create({\n    data: {\n      user_id: userId,\n      plan_id: newPlanId,\n      status: 'active',\n      billing_cycle: billingCycle,\n      current_period_start: now,\n      current_period_end: periodEnd,\n      expires_at: periodEnd,\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Cancel a user's subscription (keep active until end of billing period).\n */\nexport async function cancelSubscription(userId: string) {\n  const subscription = await db.subscription.findUnique({ where: { user_id: userId } });\n  if (!subscription) throw new Error('No subscription found');\n\n  if (subscription.plan.name === 'free') {\n    throw new Error('Cannot cancel free plan');\n  }\n\n  return db.subscription.update({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    data: {\n      status: 'cancelled',\n      cancelled_at: new Date(),\n      // Keep access until end of current period\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Downgrade a user to the free plan.\n */\nexport async function downgradeToFree(userId: string) {\n  const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n  if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found');\n\n  return db.subscription.update({\n    where: { user_id: userId },\n    data: {\n      plan_id: freePlan.id,\n      status: 'active',\n      billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      current_period_start: new Date(),\n      current_period_end: null,\n      expires_at: null,\n      cancelled_at: null,\n    },\n    include: { plan: true },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Initialize usage for a new user (called after signup).\n */\nexport async function initializeUsage(userId: string) {\n  const freePlan = await db.plan.findFirst({ where: { name: 'free' } });\n  if (!freePlan) throw new Error('Free plan not found');\n\n  await db.$transaction([\n    db.subscription.create({\n      data: {\n        user_id: userId,\n        plan_id: freePlan.id,\n        status: 'active',\n        billing_cycle: 'monthly',\n      },\n    }),\n    db.userUsage.create({\n      data: { user_id: userId },\n    }),\n  ]);\n}\n\n// Quick helper: is user on free plan?\nexport async function
